### Data/CiCdDbContext.cs (167 lines)
```csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using CiCd.Models;

namespace CiCd.Data;

public class CiCdDbContext : DbContext
{
    public CiCdDbContext(DbContextOptions<CiCdDbContext> options) : base(options) { }

    public DbSet<User> Users { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<Project> Projects { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<ProjectMember> ProjectMembers { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<Pipeline> Pipelines { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<PipelineStep> PipelineSteps { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<RunLog> RunLogs { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<StepRun> StepRuns { get; set; } = null!;
    public DbSet<Artifact> Artifacts { get; set; } = null!;

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);

        modelBuilder.Entity<User>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.Username).IsUnique();
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.Email).IsUnique();
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Project>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasOne(e => e.Owner)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(e => e.OwnerId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasMany(e => e.Members)
                .WithOne(m => m.Project)
                .HasForeignKey(m => m.ProjectId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasMany(e => e.Pipelines)
                .WithOne(p => p.Project)
                .HasForeignKey(p => p.ProjectId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Pipeline>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasMany(e => e.Steps)
                .WithOne(s => s.Pipeline)
                .HasForeignKey(s => s.PipelineId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasMany(e => e.RunLogs)
                .WithOne(r => r.Pipeline)
                .HasForeignKey(r => r.PipelineId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<PipelineStep>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasOne(e => e.LogArtifact)
                .WithOne()
                .HasForeignKey<PipelineStep>(e => e.LogArtifactId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<RunLog>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasOne(e => e.TriggeredBy)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(e => e.TriggeredByUserId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasMany(e => e.Artifacts)
                .WithOne(a => a.RunLog)
                .HasForeignKey(a => a.RunLogId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasMany(e => e.StepRuns)
                .WithOne(s => s.RunLog)
                .HasForeignKey(s => s.RunLogId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<StepRun>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasOne(e => e.Step)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(e => e.StepId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(e => e.LogArtifact)
                .WithOne()
                .HasForeignKey<StepRun>(e => e.LogArtifactId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);
        });

        SeedData(modelBuilder);
    }

    private static void SeedData(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<User>().HasData(
            new User { Id = 1, Username = "alice", Email = "alice@example.com", PasswordHash = "hash1", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 1, 10, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), Role = UserRole.Admin },
            new User { Id = 2, Username = "bob", Email = "bob@example.com", PasswordHash = "hash2", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 2, 14, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), Role = UserRole.Developer },
            new User { Id = 3, Username = "carol", Email = "carol@example.com", PasswordHash = "hash3", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 3, 5, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), Role = UserRole.Developer },
            new User { Id = 4, Username = "dave", Email = "dave@example.com", PasswordHash = "hash4", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 4, 20, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), Role = UserRole.Viewer }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<Project>().HasData(
            new Project { Id = 1, Name = "REST API", Description = "Backend REST API service", RepositoryUrl = "https://github.com/example/rest-api", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 1, 15, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), IsActive = true, OwnerId = 1 },
            new Project { Id = 2, Name = "Frontend", Description = "React/TypeScript frontend application", RepositoryUrl = "https://github.com/example/frontend", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 2, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), IsActive = true, OwnerId = 3 },
            new Project { Id = 3, Name = "Infrastructure", Description = "Terraform configs and deployment scripts", RepositoryUrl = "https://github.com/example/infra", CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 3, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), IsActive = false, OwnerId = 1 }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<ProjectMember>().HasData(
            new ProjectMember { Id = 1, UserId = 1, ProjectId = 1, Role = MemberRole.Owner, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 1, 15, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },
            new ProjectMember { Id = 2, UserId = 2, ProjectId = 1, Role = MemberRole.Contributor, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 1, 20, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },
            new ProjectMember { Id = 3, UserId = 4, ProjectId = 1, Role = MemberRole.Viewer, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 2, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },
            new ProjectMember { Id = 4, UserId = 3, ProjectId = 2, Role = MemberRole.Owner, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 2, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },
            new ProjectMember { Id = 5, UserId = 2, ProjectId = 2, Role = MemberRole.Contributor, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 2, 5, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },

            new ProjectMember { Id = 7, UserId = 3, ProjectId = 3, Role = MemberRole.Contributor, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 3, 10, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) },
            new ProjectMember { Id = 8, UserId = 4, ProjectId = 3, Role = MemberRole.Viewer, JoinedAt = new DateTime(2024, 3, 15, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<Artifact>().HasData(
            new Artifact { Id = 1, FileName = "build.log", BlobUrl = "https://s3.example.com/logs/1/build.log", SizeBytes = 4096, CreatedAt = new DateTime(2024, 5, 1, 10, 5, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), RunLogId = null },
```

**Input:**
```json
{
```
```json
  "content": "using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;\nusing CiCd.Data;\nusing CiCd.Models;\nusing CiCd.ViewModels;\n\nnamespace CiCd.Controllers;\n\npublic class ProjectController : Controller\n{\n    private readonly IProjectRepository _projectRepository;\n    private readonly IUserRepository _userRepository;\n\n    public ProjectController(IProjectRepository projectRepository, IUserRepository userRepository)\n    {\n        _projectRepository = projectRepository;\n        _userRepository = userRepository;\n    }\n\n    public IActionResult Index(string? search)\n    {\n        var projects = string.IsNullOrEmpty(search)\n            ? _projectRepository.GetAll()\n            : _projectRepository.Search(search);\n        if (Request.Headers.XRequestedWith == \"XMLHttpRequest\")\n            return Json(projects.Select(p => new {\n                p.Id, p.Name, p.Description, p.IsActive,\n                Owner = p.Owner?.Username,\n                Members = p.Members?.Count ?? 0,\n                CreatedAt = p.CreatedAt.ToString(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n            }));\n        return View(projects);\n    }\n\n    public IActionResult Details(int id)\n    {\n        var project = _projectRepository.GetById(id);\n        if (project == null) return NotFound();\n        return View(project);\n    }\n\n    public IActionResult Create()\n    {\n        ViewBag.Users = _userRepository.GetAll();\n        return View();\n    }\n\n    [HttpPost]\n    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]\n    public IActionResult Create(ProjectCreateModel model)\n    {\n        if (!ModelState.IsValid)\n        {\n            ViewBag.Users = _userRepository.GetAll();\n            return View(model);\n        }\n        var project = new Project\n        {\n            Name = model.Name,\n            Description = model.Description,\n            RepositoryUrl = model.RepositoryUrl,\n            IsActive = model.IsActive,\n            OwnerId = model.OwnerId,\n            CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow\n        };\n        _projectRepository.Add(project);\n        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));\n    }\n\n    public IActionResult Edit(int id)\n    {\n        var project = _projectRepository.GetById(id);\n        if (project == null) return NotFound();\n        ViewBag.Users = _userRepository.GetAll();\n        var model = new ProjectEditModel\n        {\n            Id = project.Id,\n            Name = project.Name,\n            Description = project.Description,\n            RepositoryUrl = project.RepositoryUrl,\n            IsActive = project.IsActive,\n            OwnerId = project.OwnerId\n        };\n        return View(model);\n    }\n\n    [HttpPost]\n    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]\n    public IActionResult Edit(int id, ProjectEditModel model)\n    {\n        if (id != model.Id) return BadRequest();\n        if (!ModelState.IsValid)\n        {\n            ViewBag.Users = _userRepository.GetAll();\n            return View(model);\n        }\n        var project = _projectRepository.GetById(id);\n        if (project == null) return NotFound();\n        if (await TryUpdateModelAsync(project, \"\", p => p.Name, p => p.Description, p => p.RepositoryUrl, p => p.IsActive, p => p.OwnerId))\n        {\n            _projectRepository.Update(project);\n            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));\n        }\n        ViewBag.Users = _userRepository.GetAll();\n        return View(model);\n    }\n\n    [HttpPost]\n    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]\n    public IActionResult Delete(int id)\n    {\n        _projectRepository.Delete(id);\n        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));\n    }\n\n    [HttpGet(\"api/users/search\")]\n    public IActionResult SearchUsers(string q)\n    {\n        var users = _userRepository.Search(q ?? \"\");\n        return Json(users.Select(u => new { u.Id, u.Username, u.Email }));\n    }\n}",
```

```json
{
  "content": "$(function () {\n    initAutocomplete();\n    initDateTimePickers();\n    initConfirmModal();\n    initDeleteForms();\n});\n\nfunction initAutocomplete() {\n    $('.autocomplete-wrapper').each(function () {\n        var wrapper = $(this);\n        var input = wrapper.find('.autocomplete-input');\n        var hidden = wrapper.find('input[type=hidden]');\n        var dropdown = wrapper.find('.autocomplete-dropdown');\n        var url = wrapper.data('search-url');\n        var timer;\n\n        input.on('input', function () {\n            clearTimeout(timer);\n            var q = $(this).val();\n            if (q.length < 2) {\n                dropdown.removeClass('open').empty();\n                return;\n            }\n            timer = setTimeout(function () {\n                $.get(url, { q: q }, function (data) {\n                    dropdown.empty();\n                    if (data.length === 0) {\n                        dropdown.removeClass('open');\n                        return;\n                    }\n                    data.forEach(function (item) {\n                        var display = item.username || item.name || item.label || item.id;\n                        var sub = item.email ? ' (' + item.email + ')' : '';\n                        dropdown.append(\n                            '<div class=\"autocomplete-item\" data-id=\"' + item.id + '\">' + display + sub + '</div>'\n                        );\n                    });\n                    dropdown.addClass('open');\n                });\n            }, 250);\n        });\n\n        dropdown.on('click', '.autocomplete-item', function () {\n            var item = $(this);\n            hidden.val(item.data('id'));\n            input.val(item.text().trim());\n            dropdown.removeClass('open').empty();\n        });\n\n        $(document).on('click', function (e) {\n            if (!$(e.target).closest(wrapper).length) {\n                dropdown.removeClass('open');\n            }\n        });\n\n        input.on('keydown', function (e) {\n            var items = dropdown.find('.autocomplete-item');\n            var selected = items.filter('.selected');\n            if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\n                e.preventDefault();\n                if (selected.length === 0) items.first().addClass('selected');\n                else { selected.removeClass('selected').next().addClass('selected'); }\n            } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\n                e.preventDefault();\n                if (selected.length === 0) items.last().addClass('selected');\n                else { selected.removeClass('selected').prev().addClass('selected'); }\n            } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n                e.preventDefault();\n                if (selected.length) selected.click();\n            } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n                dropdown.removeClass('open');\n            }\n        });\n    });\n}\n\nfunction initDateTimePickers() {\n    var locale = document.documentElement.lang || 'en';\n    if (typeof flatpickr !== 'undefined') {\n        flatpickr('.datetime-picker', {\n            enableTime: true,\n            dateFormat: 'Y-m-d H:i',\n            time_24hr: true,\n            locale: locale === 'hr' ? 'hr' : 'default',\n            theme: 'dark'\n        });\n    }\n}\n\nfunction initConfirmModal() {\n    window.showConfirm = function (message, onConfirm) {\n        var overlay = $('#confirm-modal');\n        $('#confirm-modal-message').text(message);\n        overlay.addClass('visible');\n        $('#confirm-modal-ok').off('click').on('click', function () {\n            overlay.removeClass('visible');\n            if (onConfirm) onConfirm();\n        });\n        $('#confirm-modal-cancel').off('click').on('click', function () {\n            overlay.removeClass('visible');\n        });\n    };\n}\n\nfunction initDeleteForms() {\n    $(document).on('click', '.delete-form button[data-confirm]', function (e) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        var btn = $(this);\n        var form = btn.closest('form');\n        var msg = btn.data('confirm') || 'Are you sure?';\n        if (window.showConfirm) {\n            showConfirm(msg, function () {\n                form.off('submit').submit();\n            });\n        }\n    });\n}\n\nfunction showToast(message, type) {\n    type = type || 'info';\n    var container = $('#toast-container');\n    var toast = $('<div class=\"toast toast-' + type + '\">' + message + '</div>');\n    container.append(toast);\n    setTimeout(function () {\n        toast.remove();\n    }, 3000);\n}",
```

## Assistant (Build · GLM 5.1 · 22.6s)

Build succeeds with 0 errors, 0 warnings. All Lab 4 requirements are now implemented:

- **CRUD**: Project (Create/Edit/Delete/Details), Pipeline (Create/Edit/Delete/Details), RunLog (Edit/Delete + retrigger), User (Create/Edit/soft-delete)
- **AJAX search**: All Index pages (Projects, Pipelines, Runs, Users) with debounced search + loading overlay
- **Validation**: DataAnnotations on all ViewModels + tag helpers on forms
- **Autocomplete**: `_AutocompleteDropdown.cshtml` partial + JS widget with keyboard nav
- **Date picker**: flatpickr on RunLog edit form (`.datetime-picker`) via `_DateTimePicker.cshtml` partial
- **JS animations**: Toast notifications, confirm modal for deletes, fade-in/row-highlight CSS animations, spinner
- **Soft delete**: `DeletedAt` filter on all EF repos (Project, Pipeline, RunLog, User)
- **Localization**: `app.UseRequestLocalization()` with hr + en-US cultures
